    <style>\n
        [data-invalid="true"] {\n
            border-color: var(--color-danger) !important;\n
        }\n
        [data-invalid="false"] {\n
            border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-text) 15%, transparent) !important;\n
        }\n
    </style>\n
\n
    <script>\n
        function tiyasForm(totalQuestions, initialStep, errorsFields) {\n
            return {\n
                answered: 0,\n
                totalQuestions: totalQuestions,\n
                submitting: false,\n
                currentStep: initialStep,\n
                errorFields: errorsFields,\n
                stepTitles: {\n
                    1: 'Data Identitas',\n
                    2: 'Bagian A: Iklim Transfer',\n
                    3: 'Bagian B: Lingkungan Transfer',\n
                    4: 'Bagian C: Niat Transfer',\n
                    5: 'Bagian D: Perilaku Pascapelatihan',\n
                },\n
\n
                get stepTitle() {\n
                    return this.stepTitles[this.currentStep] || '';\n
                },\n
\n
                updateProgress() {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    const radios = form.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]');\n
                    const answeredSet = new Set();\n
                    \n
                    radios.forEach(radio => {\n
                        if (radio.checked) {\n
                            answeredSet.add(radio.name);\n
                        }\n
                    });\n
                    \n
                    this.answered = answeredSet.size;\n
                    this.saveDraft();\n
                },\n
\n
                saveDraft() {\n
                    if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;\n
                    try {\n
                        const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                        const data = new FormData(form);\n
                        const draftData = {};\n
                        for (let [key, value] of data.entries()) {\n
                            // Jangan simpan kunci yang diawali "_" (_token, _method, dll)\n
                            if (!key.startsWith('_')) {\n
                                draftData[key] = value;\n
                            }\n
                        }\n
                        localStorage.setItem('tiyas-draft-1', JSON.stringify(draftData));\n
                    } catch (e) {\n
                        console.warn('Gagal menyimpan draf:', e);\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                restoreDraft() {\n
                    if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;\n
                    try {\n
                        const draft = localStorage.getItem('tiyas-draft-1');\n
                        if (draft && !this.hasErrors()) {\n
                            const draftData = JSON.parse(draft);\n
                            const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                            for (let [key, value] of Object.entries(draftData)) {\n
                                // Jangan pulihkan kunci yang diawali "_"\n
                                if (key.startsWith('_')) continue;\n
                                const field = form.elements[key];\n
                                if (field) {\n
                                    if (field.type === 'radio') {\n
                                        const radio = form.querySelector(`input[name="${key}"][value="${value}"]`);\n
                                        if (radio) radio.checked = true;\n
                                    } else {\n
                                        field.value = value;\n
                                    }\n
                                }\n
                            }\n
                            this.updateProgress();\n
                        }\n
                    } catch (e) {\n
                        console.warn('Gagal memulihkan draf:', e);\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                hasErrors() {\n
                    return this.errorFields.length > 0;\n
                },\n
\n
                nextStep() {\n
                    if (this.validateStep(this.currentStep)) {\n
                        if (this.currentStep < 5) {\n
                            this.currentStep++;\n
                            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });\n
                            });\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                previousStep() {\n
                    if (this.currentStep > 1) {\n
                        this.currentStep--;\n
                        this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });\n
                        });\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                validateStep(step) {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    const emptyFields = [];\n
\n
                    if (step === 1) {\n
                        ['asal_instansi', 'asal_kabupaten', 'jenis_profesi', 'masa_kerja', 'waktu_sejak_selesai'].forEach(field => {\n
                            const input = form.elements[field];\n
                            if (!input || !input.value) {\n
                                emptyFields.push(field);\n
                            }\n
                        });\n
                    } else {\n
                        const stepDiv = form.querySelector('[data-step="' + step + '"]');\n
                        if (stepDiv) {\n
                            const radios = stepDiv.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]');\n
                            const groupedRadios = {};\n
                            radios.forEach(radio => {\n
                                if (!groupedRadios[radio.name]) {\n
                                    groupedRadios[radio.name] = false;\n
                                }\n
                                if (radio.checked) {\n
                                    groupedRadios[radio.name] = true;\n
                                }\n
                            });\n
                            for (let name in groupedRadios) {\n
                                if (!groupedRadios[name]) {\n
                                    emptyFields.push(name);\n
                                }\n
                            }\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
\n
                    if (emptyFields.length > 0) {\n
                        // SET currentStep dulu, lalu $nextTick, baru tampilkan error\n
                        this.currentStep = step;\n
                        this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                            this.showError(emptyFields.length);\n
                            this.markErrors(emptyFields);\n
                            this.scrollToFirstError(emptyFields[0]);\n
                        });\n
                        return false;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    this.clearError();\n
                    return true;\n
                },\n
\n
                showError(count) {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    let errorDiv = form.querySelector('[data-error-message]');\n
                    if (!errorDiv) {\n
                        errorDiv = document.createElement('div');\n
                        errorDiv.setAttribute('data-error-message', 'true');\n
                        errorDiv.style.cssText = `color: var(--color-danger); font-size: 0.875rem; margin-bottom: 1rem; padding: 0.75rem; background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-danger) 10%, transparent); border-radius: 0.5rem; border-left: 3px solid var(--color-danger);`;\n
                        form.insertBefore(errorDiv, form.firstChild.nextSibling);\n
                    }\n
                    errorDiv.textContent = `Masih ada ${count} pertanyaan yang belum dijawab`;\n
                },\n
\n
                clearError() {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    const errorDiv = form.querySelector('[data-error-message]');\n
                    if (errorDiv) {\n
                        errorDiv.remove();\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                markErrors(fields) {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    // Hapus penandaan lama dari semua kartu butir\n
                    form.querySelectorAll('[data-item]').forEach(item => {\n
                        item.dataset.invalid = 'false';\n
                    });\n
                    // Tandai hanya kartu butir yang kosong\n
                    fields.forEach(fieldName => {\n
                        const itemCard = form.querySelector(`[data-item="${fieldName}"]`);\n
                        if (itemCard) {\n
                            itemCard.dataset.invalid = 'true';\n
                        }\n
                    });\n
                },\n
\n
                onItemChange(event) {\n
                    const item = event.target.closest('[data-item]');\n
                    if (!item) return;\n
                    item.dataset.invalid = 'false';\n
                    this.clearError();\n
                },\n
\n
                scrollToFirstError(fieldName) {\n
                    const form = this.$el.closest('.max-w-2xl').querySelector('form');\n
                    const itemCard = form.querySelector(`[data-item="${fieldName}"]`);\n
                    if (itemCard) {\n
                        itemCard.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                handleSubmit(event) {\n
                    if (!this.validateAllSteps()) {\n
                        event.preventDefault();\n
                        return false;\n
                    }\n
                    this.submitting = true;\n
                    this.clearDraft();\n
                },\n
\n
                validateAllSteps() {\n
                    for (let step = 1; step <= 5; step++) {\n
                        if (!this.validateStep(step)) {\n
                            // validateStep sudah set currentStep, tunggu render\n
                            return false;\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                    return true;\n
                },\n
\n
                clearDraft() {\n
                    if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return;\n
                    try {\n
                        localStorage.removeItem('tiyas-draft-1');\n
                    } catch (e) {\n
                        console.warn('Gagal menghapus draf:', e);\n
                    }\n
                },\n
\n
                init() {\n
                    this.restoreDraft();\n
                    this.updateProgress();\n
                }\n
            };\n
        }\n
    </script>\n
            </main>\n
        </div>\n
\n
            </body>\n
</html>\n
